{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CekHP4DWwJyx5MuwJZAKg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Wu1XvwErSh385wLbtx93zt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cholesky_claude-fable-5-cc-medium_cuda_r3/cholesky/cholesky.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// CUDA Cholesky decomposition (blocked right-looking algorithm)\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                              \\\n    do {                                                                              \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                    \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                    \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_),     \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                              \\\n            exit(1);                                                                  \\\n        }                                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Panel width for the blocked factorization\nconstexpr int NB = 64;\n// Tile size for the trailing-matrix update and GEMM kernels\nconstexpr int TILE = 32;\n\n// Factor the NB x NB diagonal block starting at (j0, j0) with a single thread\n// block (unblocked Cholesky in shared memory). On a non-positive-definite\n// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.\n__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double d = s[k][k];\n            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {\n                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;\n            }\n            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            const double lik = s[tid][k];\n            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {\n                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored lower triangle back\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. Each thread performs the forward\n// substitution for one row of the panel, with L11 cached in shared memory.\n__global__ void trsm_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            L[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t row = j0 + nb + (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid;\n    if (row >= n) return;\n\n    double x[NB];\n    double* a = &A[row * n + j0];\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        x[k] = a[k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        double sum = x[k];\n        for (int m = 0; m < k; ++m) {\n            sum -= x[m] * L[k][m];\n        }\n        x[k] = sum / L[k][k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        a[k] = x[k];\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update: A22 -= L21 * L21^T (lower triangle only), tiled\n// through shared memory. Blocks strictly above the diagonal exit early.\n__global__ void syrk_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double Pi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Pj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    if (blockIdx.x > blockIdx.y) return;\n\n    const size_t t0 = j0 + nb;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t row = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t col = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + tx;\n    const size_t prow = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + ty;\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        Pi[ty][tx] = (row < n) ? A[row * n + (j0 + kk + tx)] : 0.0;\n        Pj[ty][tx] = (prow < n) ? A[prow * n + (j0 + kk + tx)] : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            acc += Pi[ty][k] * Pj[tx][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (row < n && col <= row) {\n        A[row * n + col] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n// C = M * M^T for an n x n matrix (used for generation and validation)\n__global__ void mmt_kernel(double* C, const double* M, size_t n) {\n    __shared__ double Mi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Mj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t row = (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t jrow = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t col = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + tx;\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < n; kk += TILE) {\n        const size_t k0 = kk + tx;\n        Mi[ty][tx] = (row < n && k0 < n) ? M[jrow * n + k0] : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            acc += Mi[ty][k] * Mj[tx][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (row < n && col < n) {\n        C[row * n + col] = acc;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void add_diagonal_kernel(double* A, size_t n, double val) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += val;\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero out the upper triangular part\n__global__ void zero_upper_kernel(double* A, size_t n) {\n    const size_t col = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t row = (size_t)blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (row < n && col < n && col > row) {\n        A[row * n + col] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);\n\n    double* d_A = nullptr;\n    int* d_info = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_info, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_A, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_info, 0, sizeof(int)));\n\n    int info = 0;\n    for (size_t j0 = 0; On a non-positive-definite\n// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.\n__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double d = s[k][k];\n            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {\n                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;\n            }\n            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            const double lik = s[tid][k];\n            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {\n                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored lower triangle back\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. Each thread performs the forward\n// substitution for one row of the panel, with L11 cached in shared memory.\n__global__ void trsm_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            L[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t row = j0 + nb + (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid;\n    if (row >= n) return;\n\n    double x[NB];\n    double* a = &A[row * n + j0];\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        x[k] = a[k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        double sum = x[k];\n        for (int m = 0; m < k; ++m) {\n            sum -= x[m] * L[k][m];\n        }\n        x[k] = sum / L[k][k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        a[k] = x[k];\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update: A22 -= L21 * L21^T (lower triangle only), tiled\n// through shared memory. Blocks strictly above the diagonal exit early.\n__global__ void syrk_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double Pi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Pj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    if (blockIdx.x > blockIdx.y) return;\n\n    const size_t t0 = j0 + nb;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t row = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t col = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + tx;\n    const size_t prow = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + ty;\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        Pi[ty][tx] = (row < n) ? On a non-positive-definite","+// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.","+__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {","+    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];","+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;","+","+    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block","+    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {","+        const int r = idx / nb;","+        const int c = idx % nb;","+        if (c <= r) {","+            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];","+        }","+    }","+    __syncthreads();","+","+    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {","+        if (tid == 0) {","+            const double d = s[k][k];","+            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {","+                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;","+            }","+            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);","+        }","+        __syncthreads();","+        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {","+            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];","+        }","+        __syncthreads();","+        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {","+            const double lik = s[tid][k];","+            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {","+                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];","+            }","+        }","+        __syncthreads();","+    }","+","+    // Store the factored lower triangle back","+    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {","+        const int r = idx / nb;","+        const int c = idx % nb;","+        if (c <= r) {","+            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];","+        }","+    }","+}","+","+// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            \n            if (i == j) {\n                // Diagonal element\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    // Matrix is not positive definite\n                    printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n                    return false;\n                }\n                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n            } else {\n                // Off-diagonal element\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // Zero out upper triangular part\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; On a non-positive-definite\n// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.\n__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double d = s[k][k];\n            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {\n                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;\n            }\n            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            const double lik = s[tid][k];\n            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {\n                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored lower triangle back\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. Each thread performs the forward\n// substitution for one row of the panel, with L11 cached in shared memory.\n__global__ void trsm_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            L[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t row = j0 + nb + (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid;\n    if (row >= n) return;\n\n    double x[NB];\n    double* a = &A[row * n + j0];\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        x[k] = a[k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        double sum = x[k];\n        for (int m = 0; m < k; ++m) {\n            sum -= x[m] * L[k][m];\n        }\n        x[k] = sum / L[k][k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        a[k] = x[k];\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update: A22 -= L21 * L21^T (lower triangle only), tiled\n// through shared memory. Blocks strictly above the diagonal exit early.\n__global__ void syrk_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double Pi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Pj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    if (blockIdx.x > blockIdx.y) return;\n\n    const size_t t0 = j0 + nb;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t row = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t col = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + tx;\n    const size_t prow = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + ty;\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        Pi[ty][tx] = (row < n) ? A[prow * n + (j0 + kk + tx)] : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            acc += Pi[ty][k] * Pj[tx][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (row < n && col <= row) {\n        A[row * n + col] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n// C = M * M^T for an n x n matrix (used for generation and validation)\n__global__ void mmt_kernel(double* C, const double* M, size_t n) {\n    __shared__ double Mi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Mj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t row = (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t jrow = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t col = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + tx;\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < n; kk += TILE) {\n        const size_t k0 = kk + tx;\n        Mi[ty][tx] = (row < n && k0 < n) ? On a non-positive-definite\n// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.\n__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double d = s[k][k];\n            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {\n                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;\n            }\n            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            const double lik = s[tid][k];\n            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {\n                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored lower triangle back\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. Each\n// block of 16x16 threads computes a TILE x TILE output tile with 2x2 register\n// blocking; blocks strictly above the diagonal exit early.\n__global__ void syrk_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double Pi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Pj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    if (blockIdx.x > blockIdx.y) return;\n\n    const size_t t0 = j0 + nb;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;\n    const size_t ri0 = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const size_t rj0 = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc00 = 0.0, acc01 = 0.0, acc10 = 0.0, acc11 = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; ++k) {\n            const double a0 = Pi[ty][k];\n            const double a1 = Pi[ty + 16][k];\n            const double b0 = Pj[tx][k];\n            const double b1 = Pj[tx + 16][k];\n            acc00 += a0 * b0;\n            acc01 += a0 * b1;\n            acc10 += a1 * b0;\n            acc11 += a1 * b1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    const size_t row0 = ri0 + ty;\n    const size_t row1 = ri0 + ty + 16;\n    const size_t col0 = rj0 + tx;\n    const size_t col1 = rj0 + tx + 16;\n    if (row0 < n && col0 <= row0) A[row0 * n + col0] -= acc00;\n    if (row0 < n && col1 <= row0) A[row0 * n + col1] -= acc01;\n    if (row1 < n && col0 <= row1) A[row1 * n + col0] -= acc10;\n    if (row1 < n && col1 <= row1) A[row1 * n + col1] -= acc11;\n}\n\n// C = M * M^T for an n x n matrix (used for generation and validation)\n__global__ void mmt_kernel(double* C, const double* M, size_t n) {\n    __shared__ double Mi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Mj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;\n    const size_t ri0 = (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const size_t rj0 = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc00 = 0.0, acc01 = 0.0, acc10 = 0.0, acc11 = 0.0;\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < n; kk += TILE) {\n        load_tile(Mi, M, n, ri0, kk, tid, 256);\n        load_tile(Mj, M, n, rj0, kk, tid, 256);\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            const double a0 = Mi[ty][k];\n            const double a1 = Mi[ty + 16][k];\n            const double b0 = Mj[tx][k];\n            const double b1 = Mj[tx + 16][k];\n            acc00 += a0 * b0;\n            acc01 += a0 * b1;\n            acc10 += a1 * b0;\n            acc11 += a1 * b1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    const size_t row0 = ri0 + ty;\n    const size_t row1 = ri0 + ty + 16;\n    const size_t col0 = rj0 + tx;\n    const size_t col1 = rj0 + tx + 16;\n    if (row0 < n && col0 < n) C[row0 * n + col0] = acc00;\n    if (row0 < n && col1 < n) C[row0 * n + col1] = acc01;\n    if (row1 < n && col0 < n) C[row1 * n + col0] = acc10;\n    if (row1 < n && col1 < n) C[row1 * n + col1] = acc11;\n}\n\n__global__ void add_diagonal_kernel(double* A, size_t n, double val) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += val;\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero out the upper triangular part\n__global__ void zero_upper_kernel(double* A, size_t n) {\n    const size_t col = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t row = (size_t)blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (row < n && col < n && col > row) {\n        A[row * n + col] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);\n\n    double* d_A = nullptr;\n    int* d_info = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_info, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_A, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_info, 0, sizeof(int)));\n\n    int info = 0;\n    for (size_t j0 = 0;                                                    \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                    \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_),     \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__); On a non-positive-definite\n// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.\n__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double d = s[k][k];\n            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {\n                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;\n            }\n            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            const double lik = s[tid][k];\n            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {\n                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored lower triangle back\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. Each thread performs the forward\n// substitution for one row of the panel, with L11 cached in shared memory.\n__global__ void trsm_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            L[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t row = j0 + nb + (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid;\n    if (row >= n) return;\n\n    double x[NB];\n    double* a = &A[row * n + j0];\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        x[k] = a[k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        double sum = x[k];\n        for (int m = 0; m < k; ++m) {\n            sum -= x[m] * L[k][m];\n        }\n        x[k] = sum / L[k][k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        a[k] = x[k];\n    }\n}\n\n// Cooperative load of a TILE x TILE tile of rows [r0, r0+TILE) x columns\n// [c0, c0+TILE) into shared memory, zero-padded outside the matrix.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void load_tile(double dst[TILE][TILE + 1], const double* A,\n                                          size_t n, size_t r0, size_t c0, int tid,\n                                          int nthreads) {\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < TILE * TILE; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / TILE;\n        const int c = idx % TILE;\n        const size_t row = r0 + r;\n        const size_t col = c0 + c;\n        dst[r][c] = (row < n && col < n) ? A[row * n + col] : 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update: A22 -= L21 * L21^T (lower triangle only). Each\n// block of 16x16 threads computes a TILE x TILE output tile with 2x2 register\n// blocking; blocks strictly above the diagonal exit early.\n__global__ void syrk_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double Pi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Pj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    if (blockIdx.x > blockIdx.y) return;\n\n    const size_t t0 = j0 + nb;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;\n    const size_t ri0 = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const size_t rj0 = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc00 = 0.0, acc01 = 0.0, acc10 = 0.0, acc11 = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        load_tile(Pi, A, n, ri0, j0 + kk, tid, 256);\n        load_tile(Pj, A, n, rj0, j0 + kk, tid, 256);\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE;                                                    \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                    \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_),     \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__); On a non-positive-definite\n// diagonal, records the 1-based global index of the first failure in *info.\n__global__ void potf2_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle (and diagonal) of the block\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            s[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double d = s[k][k];\n            if (d <= 0.0 && *info == 0) {\n                *info = (int)(j0 + k) + 1;\n            }\n            s[k][k] = sqrt(d);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            s[tid][k] /= s[k][k];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid > k && tid < nb) {\n            const double lik = s[tid][k];\n            for (int j = k + 1; j <= tid; ++j) {\n                s[tid][j] -= lik * s[j][k];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored lower triangle back\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: L21 = A21 * L11^{-T}. Each thread performs the forward\n// substitution for one row of the panel, with L11 cached in shared memory.\n__global__ void trsm_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB + 1];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx % nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            L[r][c] = A[(j0 + r) * n + (j0 + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t row = j0 + nb + (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid;\n    if (row >= n) return;\n\n    double x[NB];\n    double* a = &A[row * n + j0];\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        x[k] = a[k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        double sum = x[k];\n        for (int m = 0; m < k; ++m) {\n            sum -= x[m] * L[k][m];\n        }\n        x[k] = sum / L[k][k];\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n        a[k] = x[k];\n    }\n}\n\n// Cooperative load of a TILE x TILE tile of rows [r0, r0+TILE) x columns\n// [c0, c0+TILE) into shared memory, zero-padded outside the matrix.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void load_tile(double dst[TILE][TILE + 1], const double* A,\n                                          size_t n, size_t r0, size_t c0, int tid,\n                                          int nthreads) {\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < TILE * TILE; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / TILE;\n        const int c = idx % TILE;\n        const size_t row = r0 + r;\n        const size_t col = c0 + c;\n        dst[r][c] = (row < n && col < n) ? A[row * n + col] : 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update: A22 -= L21 * L21^T (lower triangle only). Each\n// block of 16x16 threads computes a TILE x TILE output tile with 2x2 register\n// blocking; blocks strictly above the diagonal exit early.\n__global__ void syrk_kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t j0, int nb) {\n    __shared__ double Pi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Pj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    if (blockIdx.x > blockIdx.y) return;\n\n    const size_t t0 = j0 + nb;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;\n    const size_t ri0 = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const size_t rj0 = t0 + (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc00 = 0.0, acc01 = 0.0, acc10 = 0.0, acc11 = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        load_tile(Pi, A, n, ri0, j0 + kk, tid, 256);\n        load_tile(Pj, A, n, rj0, j0 + kk, tid, 256);\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            const double a0 = Pi[ty][k];\n            const double a1 = Pi[ty + 16][k];\n            const double b0 = Pj[tx][k];\n            const double b1 = Pj[tx + 16][k];\n            acc00 += a0 * b0;\n            acc01 += a0 * b1;\n            acc10 += a1 * b0;\n            acc11 += a1 * b1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    const size_t row0 = ri0 + ty;\n    const size_t row1 = ri0 + ty + 16;\n    const size_t col0 = rj0 + tx;\n    const size_t col1 = rj0 + tx + 16;\n    if (row0 < n && col0 <= row0) A[row0 * n + col0] -= acc00;\n    if (row0 < n && col1 <= row0) A[row0 * n + col1] -= acc01;\n    if (row1 < n && col0 <= row1) A[row1 * n + col0] -= acc10;\n    if (row1 < n && col1 <= row1) A[row1 * n + col1] -= acc11;\n}\n\n// C = M * M^T for an n x n matrix (used for generation and validation)\n__global__ void mmt_kernel(double* C, const double* M, size_t n) {\n    __shared__ double Mi[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Mj[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;\n    const size_t ri0 = (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const size_t rj0 = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc00 = 0.0, acc01 = 0.0, acc10 = 0.0, acc11 = 0.0;\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < n; kk += TILE) {\n        load_tile(Mi, M, n, ri0, kk, tid, 256);\n        load_tile(Mj, M, n, rj0, kk, tid, 256);\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n            const double a0 = Mi[ty][k];\n            const double a1 = Mi[ty + 16][k];\n            const double b0 = Mj[tx][k];\n            const double b1 = Mj[tx + 16][k];\n            acc00 += a0 * b0;\n            acc01 += a0 * b1;\n            acc10 += a1 * b0;\n            acc11 += a1 * b1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    const size_t row0 = ri0 + ty;\n    const size_t row1 = ri0 + ty + 16;\n    const size_t col0 = rj0 + tx;\n    const size_t col1 = rj0 + tx + 16;\n    if (row0 < n && col0 < n) C[row0 * n + col0] = acc00;\n    if (row0 < n && col1 < n) C[row0 * n + col1] = acc01;\n    if (row1 < n && col0 < n) C[row1 * n + col0] = acc10;\n    if (row1 < n && col1 < n) C[row1 * n + col1] = acc11;\n}\n\n__global__ void add_diagonal_kernel(double* A, size_t n, double val) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += val;\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero out the upper triangular part\n__global__ void zero_upper_kernel(double* A, size_t n) {\n    const size_t col = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t row = (size_t)blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (row < n && col < n && col > row) {\n        A[row * n + col] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);\n\n    double* d_A = nullptr;\n    int* d_info = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_info, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_A, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_info, 0, sizeof(int)));\n\n    int info = 0;\n    for (size_t j0 = 0; j0 < n; j0 += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - j0);\n\n        potf2_kernel<<<1, NB>>>(d_A, n, j0, nb, d_info);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&info, d_info, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        if (info != 0) {\n            // Matrix is not positive definite\n            printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n                   (size_t)(info - 1));\n            cudaFree(d_A);\n            cudaFree(d_info);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        const size_t m = n - j0 - nb; // rows below the panel\n        if (m > 0) {\n            const int threads = 128;\n            const unsigned blocks = (unsigned)((m + threads - 1) / threads);\n            trsm_kernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(d_A, n, j0, nb);\n\n            const unsigned tiles = (unsigned)((m + TILE - 1) / TILE);\n            dim3 grid(tiles, tiles);\n            dim3 block(16, 16);\n            syrk_kernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, n, j0, nb);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    {\n        dim3 block(32, 8);\n        dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + 31) / 32), (unsigned)((n + 7) / 8));\n        zero_upper_kernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, n);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), d_A, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_A));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_info));\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU\n    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_B = nullptr;\n    double* d_A = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_B, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_B, B.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    dim3 block(16, 16);\n    dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + TILE - 1) / TILE), (unsigned)((n + TILE - 1) / TILE));\n    mmt_kernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, d_B, n);\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    add_diagonal_kernel<<<(unsigned)((n + 255) / 256), 256>>>(d_A, n, (double)n);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), d_A, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_B));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_A));\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T on the GPU\n    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_L = nullptr;\n    double* d_R = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_L, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_R, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_L, L.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    dim3 block(TILE, TILE);\n    dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + TILE - 1) / TILE), (unsigned)((n + TILE - 1) / TILE));\n    mmt_kernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_R, d_L, n);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(reconstructed.data(), d_R, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_L));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_R));\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Initialize the CUDA context up front so it is not part of the timing\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));\n\n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n\n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n\n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n\n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":329,"oldLines":7,"newStart":329,"newLines":7,"lines":["     CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_R, bytes));","     CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_L, L.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));"," ","-    dim3 block(TILE, TILE);","+    dim3 block(16, 16);","     dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + TILE - 1) / TILE), (unsigned)((n + TILE - 1) / TILE));","     mmt_kernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_R, d_L, n);"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
